# cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    69\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    70\t 91\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n    92\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n    93\t        \n    94\t        # Evaluate initial population\n 179\t            \n   180\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.stats = {\n   183\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   184\t            'gbest_f': 'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   189\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   190\t        }\n   191\t reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    23\t            for row in reader:\n    24\t                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n    25\t                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    26\t    return opt_vals\n    27\t\n    28\t\n    29\tdef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    30\t    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    31\t    \n    32\t    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    33\t    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    34\t    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    35\t    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    36\t    \n    37\t    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    38\t    \"\"\"    \n    39\t    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n    40\t        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    41\t    \n    42\t    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    43\t    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n    44\t\n    45\t\n    46\tdef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    47\t    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    48\t    \n    49\t    Args:\n    50\t        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n    51\t        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n    52\t        log_fn: logging function\n    53\t        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n    54\t        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n    55\t        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    56\t    \n    57\t    Returns:\n    58\t        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    59\t    \"\"\"\n    60\t    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    61\t    import cocoex\n    62\t\n    63\t    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    64\t    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    65\t    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n    66\t        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    67\t    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    68\t    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    69\t    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    70\t    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    71\t    \n    72\t    # Create or use provided controller\n    73\t    if controller is None:\n    74\t        # load meta learning implementations\n    75\t        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n    76\t        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n    77\t        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n    78\t        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n    79\t        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    80\t    \n    81\t    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    82\t    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    83\t    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    84\t    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n    85\t\n    86\t    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    87\t    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    88\t    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n    89\t\n    90\t    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    91\t    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    92\t    problem = next(iter(suite))\n    93\t\n    94\t    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    95\t    try:\n    96\t        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n    97\t        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n    98\t        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    99\t        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n   100\t        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n   101\t        \n   102\t        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n   103\t        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n   104\t            import torch\n   105\t            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n   106\t            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n   107\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n   108\t    except Exception as e:\n   109\t        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n   110\t\n   111\t    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n   112\t    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n   113\t    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n   114\t    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n   115\t    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n   116\t    \n   117\t    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n   118\t    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n   119\t    \n   120\t    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n   121\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n   122\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n   123\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n   124\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n   125\t    \n   126\t    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n   127\t    if mode == 'train':\n   128\t        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n   129\t        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n   130\t        if training_loss is not None:\n   131\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n   132\t        if sweep_return is not None:\n   133\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n   134\t        return {\n   135\t            'score': score,\n   136\t            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   137\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   138\t            'opt_val': opt_val,\n   139\t            'training_loss': training_loss,\n   140\t            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n   141\t            'controller': controller\n   142\t        }\n   143\t    \n   144\t    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n   145\t    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n   146\t\n   147\t    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n   148\t    return {\n   149\t Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

\u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n\\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n            # Population sorted by fitness: used to select pbest members.\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')\\n            top_n = max(1, int(np.ceil(pbest_ratio * pop_size)))\\n\\n            # Pre-build archive array once per iteration.\\n            if archive:\\n                archive_arr = np.asarray(archive, dtype=float)\\n            else:\\n                archive_arr = None\\n\\n            trial_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\\n\\n            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Select pbest (from the best pbest_ratio individuals)\\n                top = order[:top_n]\\n                top_excl = top[top != i] if len(top) > 1 else top\\n                if len(top_excl) == 0:\\n                    pbest_idx = i\\n                else:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(top_excl[rng.randint(len(top_excl))])\\n\\n                # Select a random individual from the main population\\n                # (different from current and pbest).\\n                idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i and j != pbest_idx]\\n                if not idx_pool:\\n                    idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\\n                idx1 = int(idx_pool[rng.randint(len(idx_pool))])\\n\\n                # Select the difference vector term from population + archive.\\n                # This improves diversity and retains information from discarded solutions.\\n                if archive_arr is not None:\\n                    total = pop_size + len(archive_arr)\\n                    attempts = 0\\n                    while True:\\n                        u = int(rng.randint(total))\\n                        same_as_current = (u == i) or (u == i - total)  # negative archive index cannot equal i, but safe\\n                        same_as_pbest = (u == pbest_idx) and (u < pop_size)\\n                        same_as_x1 = (u == idx1) and (u < pop_size)\\n                        if (not same_as_current and not same_as_pbest and not same_as_x1) or attempts >= 20:\\n                            break\\n                        attempts += 1\\n                    if u < pop_size:\\n                        diff_vec = pop[u]\\n                    else:\\n                        diff_vec = archive_arr[u - pop_size]\\n                else:\\n                    idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size)\\n                                 if j != i and j != pbest_idx and j != idx1]\\n                    if not idx_pool2:\\n                        idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\\n                    idx2 = int(idx_pool2[rng.randint(len(idx_pool2))])\\n                    diff_vec = pop[idx2]\\n\\n                # Adaptive F and CR from memory (Cauchy and Normal sampling)\\n                mem_idx = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\\n                F_i = float(mu_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * np.tan(np.pi * (rng.random() - 0.5)))\\n                CR_i = float(mu_CR[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.randn())\\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = 0.1\\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\\n                    F_i = 1.0\\n                if CR_i < 0.0:\\n                    CR_i = 0.0\\n                elif CR_i > 1.0:\\n                    CR_i = 1.0\\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[idx1] - diff_vec)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: reflect out-of-bound dimensions back into the box\\n                low_mask = trial < lb\\n                high_mask = trial > ub\\n                trial[low_mask] = 0.5 * (lb[low_mask] + pop[i][low_mask])\\n                trial[high_mask] = 0.5 * (ub[high_mask] + pop[i][high_mask])\\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\\n\\n                trial_pop[i] = trial\\n\\n            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\\n            F_success = []\\n            CR_success = []\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Add the discarded solution to the archive (bounded size)\\n                    if len(archive) >= archive_capacity:\\n                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(len(archive))))\\n                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\\n\\n                    pop[i] = trial_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    F_success.append(float(F_vals[i]))\\n                    CR_success.append(float(CR_vals[i]))\\n\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = trial_pop[i].copy()\\n\\n            # Adapt F/CR memories based on successful values\\n            if F_success:\\n                f_arr = np.asarray(F_success, dtype=float)\\n                cr_arr = np.asarray(CR_success, dtype=float)\\n                denom = float(np.sum(f_arr))\\n                if denom > 1e-12:\\n                    # Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic mean for CR\\n                    mu_F[memory_pos] = float(np.sum(f_arr ** 2) / denom)\\n                    mu_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.mean(cr_arr))\\n                    memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_size\\n\\n            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n\\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n\\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\"\n    }\n}\n</json>",

Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\\n            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\\n            \\n            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \\n                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\\n            \\n            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\", 'new_str': \"        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\\n        if x0 is not None:\\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float)\\n            if x0_arr.ndim == 1 and x0_arr.shape[0] == self.dim:\\n                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\\n\\n        # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n\\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n\\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # ---------------- JADE-style adaptive differential evolution ----------------\\n        # JADE uses a p-best mutation (current-to-pbest/1), an external archive of\\n        # discarded solutions, and self-adaptive F/CR memories (Cauchy/Normal).\\n        memory_size = 10\\n        mu_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        mu_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        memory_pos = 0\\n        archive = []\\n        archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 2 * pop_size)\\n        pbest_ratio = 0.1\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n\\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n            # Population sorted by fitness: used to select pbest members.\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')\\n            top_n = max(1, int(np.ceil(pbest_ratio * pop_size)))\\n\\n            # Pre-build archive array once per iteration.\\n            if archive:\\n                archive_arr = np.asarray(archive, dtype=float)\\n            else:\\n                archive_arr = None\\n\\n            trial_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\\n\\n            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Select pbest (from the best pbest_ratio individuals)\\n                top = order[:top_n]\\n                top_excl = top[top != i] if len(top) > 1 else top\\n                if len(top_excl) == 0:\\n                    pbest_idx = i\\n                else:\\n                    pbest_idx = int(top_excl[rng.randint(len(top_excl))])\\n\\n                # Select a random individual from the main population\\n                # (different from current and pbest).\\n                idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i and j != pbest_idx]\\n                if not idx_pool:\\n                    idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\\n                idx1 = int(idx_pool[rng.randint(len(idx_pool))])\\n\\n                # Select the difference vector term from population + archive.\\n                # This improves diversity and retains information from discarded solutions.\\n                if archive_arr is not None:\\n                    total = pop_size + len(archive_arr)\\n                    attempts = 0\\n                    while True:\\n                        u = int(rng.randint(total))\\n                        same_as_current = (u == i) or (u == i - total)  # negative archive index cannot equal i, but safe\\n                        same_as_pbest = (u == pbest_idx) and (u < pop_size)\\n                        same_as_x1 = (u == idx1) and (u < pop_size)\\n                        if (not same_as_current and not same_as_pbest and not same_as_x1) or attempts >= 20:\\n                            break\\n                        attempts += 1\\n                    if u < pop_size:\\n                        diff_vec = pop[u]\\n                    else:\\n                        diff_vec = archive_arr[u - pop_size]\\n                else:\\n                    idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size)\\n                                 if j != i and j != pbest_idx and j != idx1]\\n                    if not idx_pool2:\\n                        idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\\n                    idx2 = int(idx_pool2[rng.randint(len(idx_pool2))])\\n                    diff_vec = pop[idx2]\\n\\n                # Adaptive F and CR from memory (Cauchy and Normal sampling)\\n                mem_idx = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\\n                F_i = float(mu_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * np.tan(np.pi * (rng.random() - 0.5)))\\n                CR_i = float(mu_CR[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.randn())\\n                if F_i <= 0.0:\\n                    F_i = 0.1\\n                elif F_i > 1.0:\\n                    F_i = 1.0\\n                if CR_i < 0.0:\\n                    CR_i = 0.0\\n                elif CR_i > 1.0:\\n                    CR_i = 1.0\\n                F_vals[i] = F_i\\n                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[idx1] - diff_vec)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\\n                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\\n                mask[j_rand] = True\\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: reflect out-of-bound dimensions back into the box\\n                low_mask = trial < lb\\n                high_mask = trial > ub\\n                trial[low_mask] = 0.5 * (lb[low_mask] + pop[i][low_mask])\\n                trial[high_mask] = 0.5 * (ub[high_mask] + pop[i][high_mask])\\n                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\\n\\n                trial_pop[i] = trial\\n\\n            # Adapt F/CR memories based on successful values\\n            if F_success:\\n                f_arr = np.asarray(F_success, dtype=float)\\n                cr_arr = np.asarray(CR_success, dtype=float)\\n                denom = float(np.sum(f_arr))\\n                if denom > 1e-12:\\n                    # Boundary handling: clip\\n                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\\n            \\n            # Evaluate trial vectors\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\\n                evals += 1\\n                \\n                # Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\\n            \\n            # Evaluate initial population\\n        evals = 0\\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\\n\\n        for i in range(pop_size):\\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                break\\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\\n            evals += 1\\n\\n        # Track best\\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\\n\\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\\n        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\\n        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\\n\\n        # ---------------- JADE-style adaptive differential evolution ----------------\\n        # JADE uses a p-best mutation (current-to-pbest/1), an external archive of\\n        # discarded solutions, and self-adaptive F/CR memories (Cauchy/Normal).\\n        memory_size = 10\\n        mu_F = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        mu_CR = np.full(memory_size, 0.5)\\n        memory_pos = 0\\n        archive = []\\n        archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 2 * pop_size)\\n        pbest_ratio = 0.1\\n\\n        # Main loop\\n        iteration = 0\\n        done = False\\n\\n        while not done:\\n            iteration += 1\\n            params = dict(self.params)\\n            if self.controller is not None:\\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\\n                    params.update(step_params)\\n\\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\\n\\n            # Population sorted by fitness: used to select pbest members.\\n            order = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')\\n            top_n = max(1, int(np.ceil(pbest_ratio * pop_size)))\\n\\n            # Pre-build archive array once per iteration.\\n            if archive:\\n                archive_arr = np.asarray(archive, dtype=float)\\n            else:\\n                archive_arr = None\\n\\n            trial_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\\n            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\\n            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\\n\\n            # Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"editor\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': 'view', 'path': '/hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py', 'view_range': [80, -1]},\n        \"tool_output\": \"Here's the result of running `cat -n` on /hada/metabbo/ec_algorithm.py:\n    80\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    81\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    82\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    83\t\n    84\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    85\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    86\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    87\t        \n    88\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    89\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    90\t\n    91\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n    92\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n    93\t        if x0 is not None:\n    94\t            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float)\n    95\t            if x0_arr.ndim == 1 and x0_arr.shape[0] == self.dim:\n    96\t                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n    97\t\n    98\t        # Evaluate initial population\n    99\t        evals = 0\n   100\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   101\t\n   102\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   103\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   104\t                break\n   105\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   106\t            evals += 1\n   107\t\n   108\t        # Track best\n   109\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   110\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   111\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   112\t\n   113\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   114\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   115\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   116\t\n   117\t        # ---------------- JADE-style adaptive differential evolution ----------------\n   118\t        # JADE uses a p-best mutation (current-to-pbest/1), an external archive of\n   119\t        # discarded solutions, and self-adaptive F/CR memories (Cauchy/Normal).\n   120\t        memory_size = 10\n   121\t        base_F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n   122\t        base_CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n   123\t        mu_F = np.full(memory_size, base_F)\n   124\t        mu_CR = np.full(memory_size, base_CR)\n   125\t        memory_pos = 0\n   126\t        archive = []\n   127\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 2 * pop_size)\n   128\t        pbest_ratio = 0.1\n   129\t\n   130\t        # Main loop\n   131\t        iteration = 0\n   132\t        done = False\n   133\t\n   134\t        while not done:\n   135\t            iteration += 1\n   136\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   137\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   138\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   139\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   140\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   141\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   142\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   143\t\n   144\t            # The controller can adapt the JADE p-best ratio and archive size.\n   145\t            pbest_ratio = max(0.02, min(0.5, float(params.get('pbest_ratio', 0.1))))\n   146\t            archive_capacity = max(pop_size, int(pop_size * float(params.get('archive_mult', 2.0))))\n   147\t\n   148\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   149\t\n   150\t            # Population sorted by fitness: used to select pbest members.\n   151\t            order = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')\n   152\t            top_n = max(1, int(np.ceil(pbest_ratio * pop_size)))\n   153\t\n   154\t            # Pre-build archive array once per iteration.\n   155\t            if archive:\n   156\t                archive_arr = np.asarray(archive, dtype=float)\n   157\t            else:\n   158\t                archive_arr = None\n   159\t\n   160\t            trial_pop = np.empty_like(pop)\n   161\t            F_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   162\t            CR_vals = np.zeros(pop_size)\n   163\t\n   164\t            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1\n   165\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   166\t                # Select pbest (from the best pbest_ratio individuals)\n   167\t                top = order[:top_n]\n   168\t                top_excl = top[top != i] if len(top) > 1 else top\n   169\t                if len(top_excl) == 0:\n   170\t                    pbest_idx = i\n   171\t                else:\n   172\t                    pbest_idx = int(top_excl[rng.randint(len(top_excl))])\n   173\t\n   174\t                # Select a random individual from the main population\n   175\t                # (different from current and pbest).\n   176\t                idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i and j != pbest_idx]\n   177\t                if not idx_pool:\n   178\t                    idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\n   179\t                idx1 = int(idx_pool[rng.randint(len(idx_pool))])\n   180\t\n   181\t                # Select the difference vector term from population + archive.\n   182\t                # This improves diversity and retains information from discarded solutions.\n   183\t                if archive_arr is not None:\n   184\t                    total = pop_size + len(archive_arr)\n   185\t                    attempts = 0\n   186\t                    while True:\n   187\t                        u = int(rng.randint(total))\n   188\t                        same_as_current = (u == i) or (u == i - total)  # negative archive index cannot equal i, but safe\n   189\t                        same_as_pbest = (u == pbest_idx) and (u < pop_size)\n   190\t                        same_as_x1 = (u == idx1) and (u < pop_size)\n   191\t                        if (not same_as_current and not same_as_pbest and not same_as_x1) or attempts >= 20:\n   192\t                            break\n   193\t                        attempts += 1\n   194\t                    if u < pop_size:\n   195\t                        diff_vec = pop[u]\n   196\t                    else:\n   197\t                        diff_vec = archive_arr[u - pop_size]\n   198\t                else:\n   199\t                    idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size)\n   200\t                                 if j != i and j != pbest_idx and j != idx1]\n   201\t                    if not idx_pool2:\n   202\t                        idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\n   203\t                    idx2 = int(idx_pool2[rng.randint(len(idx_pool2))])\n   204\t                    diff_vec = pop[idx2]\n   205\t\n   206\t                # Adaptive F and CR from memory (Cauchy and Normal sampling)\n   207\t                mem_idx = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n   208\t                F_i = float(mu_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * np.tan(np.pi * (rng.random() - 0.5)))\n   209\t                CR_i = float(mu_CR[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.randn())\n   210\t                if F_i <= 0.0:\n   211\t                    F_i = 0.1\n   212\t                elif F_i > 1.0:\n   213\t                    F_i = 1.0\n   214\t                if CR_i < 0.0:\n   215\t                    CR_i = 0.0\n   216\t                elif CR_i > 1.0:\n   217\t                    CR_i = 1.0\n   218\t                F_vals[i] = F_i\n   219\t                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\n   220\t\n   221\t                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   222\t                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[idx1] - diff_vec)\n   223\t\n   224\t                # Binomial crossover\n   225\t                j_rand = int(rng.randint(self.dim))\n   226\t                mask = rng.random(self.dim) < CR_i\n   227\t                mask[j_rand] = True\n   228\t                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n   229\t\n   230\t                # Boundary handling: reflect out-of-bound dimensions back into the box\n   231\t                low_mask = trial < lb\n   232\t                high_mask = trial > ub\n   233\t                trial[low_mask] = 0.5 * (lb[low_mask] + pop[i][low_mask])\n   234\t                trial[high_mask] = 0.5 * (ub[high_mask] + pop[i][high_mask])\n   235\t                trial = np.clip(trial, lb, ub)\n   236\t\n   237\t                trial_pop[i] = trial\n   238\t\n   239\t            # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n   240\t            F_success = []\n   241\t            CR_success = []\n   242\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   243\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   244\t                    done = True\n   245\t                    break\n   246\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial_pop[i])\n   247\t                evals += 1\n   248\t\n   249\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   250\t                    # Add the discarded solution to the archive (bounded size)\n   251\t                    if len(archive) >= archive_capacity:\n   252\t                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(len(archive))))\n   253\t                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n   254\t\n   255\t                    pop[i] = trial_pop[i].copy()\n   256\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   257\t                    F_success.append(float(F_vals[i]))\n   258\t                    CR_success.append(float(CR_vals[i]))\n   259\t\n   260\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   261\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   262\t                        gbest = trial_pop[i].copy()\n   263\t\n   264\t            # Adapt F/CR memories based on successful values\n   265\t            if F_success:\n   266\t                f_arr = np.asarray(F_success, dtype=float)\n   267\t                cr_arr = np.asarray(CR_success, dtype=float)\n   268\t                denom = float(np.sum(f_arr))\n   269\t                if denom > 1e-12:\n   270\t                    # Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic mean for CR\n   271\t                    mu_F[memory_pos] = float(np.sum(f_arr ** 2) / denom)\n   272\t                    mu_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.mean(cr_arr))\n   273\t                    memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_size\n   274\t\n   275\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   276\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   277\t\n   278\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   279\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   280\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   281\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   282\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   283\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   284\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   285\t\n   286\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   287\t\n   288\t        self.stats = {\n   289\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   290\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   291\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   292\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   293\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   294\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   295\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   296\t        }\n   297\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

---------------- JADE-style adaptive differential evolution ----------------\n   118\t        # JADE uses a p-best mutation (current-to-pbest/1), an external archive of\n   119\t        # discarded solutions, and self-adaptive F/CR memories (Cauchy/Normal).\n   120\t        memory_size = 10\n   121\t        base_F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n   122\t        base_CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n   123\t        mu_F = np.full(memory_size, base_F)\n   124\t        mu_CR = np.full(memory_size, base_CR)\n   125\t        memory_pos = 0\n   126\t        archive = []\n   127\t        archive_capacity = max(pop_size, 2 * pop_size)\n   128\t        pbest_ratio = 0.1\n   129\t\n   130\t        # Population sorted by fitness: used to select pbest members.\n   151\t            order = np.argsort(fitness, kind='stable')\n   152\t            top_n = max(1, int(np.ceil(pbest_ratio * pop_size)))\n   153\t\n   154\t            # Generate trial vectors using current-to-pbest/1\n   165\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   166\t                # Select pbest (from the best pbest_ratio individuals)\n   167\t                top = order[:top_n]\n   168\t                top_excl = top[top != i] if len(top) > 1 else top\n   169\t                if len(top_excl) == 0:\n   170\t                    pbest_idx = i\n   171\t                else:\n   172\t                    pbest_idx = int(top_excl[rng.randint(len(top_excl))])\n   173\t\n   174\t                # Select a random individual from the main population\n   175\t                # (different from current and pbest).\n   176\t                idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i and j != pbest_idx]\n   177\t                if not idx_pool:\n   178\t                    idx_pool = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\n   179\t                idx1 = int(idx_pool[rng.randint(len(idx_pool))])\n   180\t\n   181\t                # Select the difference vector term from population + archive.\n   182\t                # This improves diversity and retains information from discarded solutions.\n   183\t                if archive_arr is not None:\n   184\t                    total = pop_size + len(archive_arr)\n   185\t                    attempts = 0\n   186\t                    while True:\n   187\t                        u = int(rng.randint(total))\n   188\t                        same_as_current = (u == i) or (u == i - total)  # negative archive index cannot equal i, but safe\n   189\t                        same_as_pbest = (u == pbest_idx) and (u < pop_size)\n   190\t                        same_as_x1 = (u == idx1) and (u < pop_size)\n   191\t                        if (not same_as_current and not same_as_pbest and not same_as_x1) or attempts >= 20:\n   192\t                            break\n   193\t                        attempts += 1\n   194\t                    if u < pop_size:\n   195\t                        diff_vec = pop[u]\n   196\t                    else:\n   197\t                        diff_vec = archive_arr[u - pop_size]\n   198\t                else:\n   199\t                    idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size)\n   200\t                                 if j != i and j != pbest_idx and j != idx1]\n   201\t                    if not idx_pool2:\n   202\t                        idx_pool2 = [j for j in range(pop_size) if j != i]\n   203\t                    idx2 = int(idx_pool2[rng.randint(len(idx_pool2))])\n   204\t                    diff_vec = pop[idx2]\n   205\t\n   206\t                # Adaptive F and CR from memory (Cauchy and Normal sampling)\n   207\t                mem_idx = int(rng.randint(memory_size))\n   208\t                F_i = float(mu_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * np.tan(np.pi * (rng.random() - 0.5)))\n   209\t                CR_i = float(mu_CR[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.randn())\n   210\t                if F_i <= 0.0:\n   211\t                    F_i = 0.1\n   212\t                elif F_i > 1.0:\n   213\t                    F_i = 1.0\n   214\t                if CR_i < 0.0:\n   215\t                    CR_i = 0.0\n   216\t                elif CR_i > 1.0:\n   217\t                    CR_i = 1.0\n   218\t                F_vals[i] = F_i\n   219\t                CR_vals[i] = CR_i\n   220\t\n   221\t                # Evaluate trial vectors and apply greedy selection\n   240\t            F_success = []\n   241\t            CR_success = []\n   242\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   243\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   244\t                    done = True\n   245\t                    break\n   246\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial_pop[i])\n   247\t                evals += 1\n   248\t\n   249\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   250\t                    # Add the discarded solution to the archive (bounded size)\n   251\t                    if len(archive) >= archive_capacity:\n   252\t                        archive.pop(int(rng.randint(len(archive))))\n   253\t                    archive.append(pop[i].copy())\n   254\t\n   255\t                    pop[i] = trial_pop[i].copy()\n   256\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   257\t                    F_success.append(float(F_vals[i]))\n   258\t                    CR_success.append(float(CR_vals[i]))\n   259\t\n   260\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   261\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   262\t                        gbest = trial_pop[i].copy()\n   263\t\n   264\t            # Adapt F/CR memories based on successful values\n   265\t            if F_success:\n   266\t                f_arr = np.asarray(F_success, dtype=float)\n   267\t                cr_arr = np.asarray(CR_success, dtype=float)\n   268\t                denom = float(np.sum(f_arr))\n   269\t                if denom > 1e-12:\n   270\t                    # Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic mean for CR\n   271\t                    mu_F[memory_pos] = float(np.sum(f_arr ** 2) / denom)\n   272\t                    mu_CR[memory_pos] = float(np.mean(cr_arr))\n   273\t                    memory_pos = (memory_pos + 1) % memory_size\n   274\t\n   275\t            #
